The original design forced all display settings into a single string value, making it\n// difficult to add new customization parameters or provide mode-specific configuration options.\n//\n// The migration works by:\n// 1. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01Hfe6vfYZYZNpjmxPRPEqSD","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012E1Gj6MTH4PVtvk9dbnnua","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/investigation.md","content":"# Investigation Report: Dashboard Migration v38 Table Panel Regression\n\n## Summary\n\nThe regression is caused by a timing mismatch in the **v38 migration function** (`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go:75-132`). When a table panel lacks an explicit `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` object in the original dashboard JSON, the v38 migration fails to process the field overrides that contain `custom.displayMode` properties. However, a separate cleanup function later removes these unprocessed override entries because they are treated as default values, resulting in lost column formatting configurations.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n**Location:** `apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go:115-127` (v38 migration function)\n\n**Conditional Logic Error:**\n\n```go\n// Process defaults.custom if it exists\nif defaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n    if custom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n        // Migrate displayMode to cellOptions in defaults\n        if displayMode, exists := custom[\"displayMode\"]; exists {\n            if displayModeStr, ok := displayMode.(string); ok {\n                custom[\"cellOptions\"] = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(displayModeStr)\n            }\n            delete(custom, \"displayMode\")\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Update any overrides referencing the cell display mode\n// This must be called regardless of whether defaults.custom exists\nmigrateOverrides(fieldConfig)  // Line 131\n```\n\nThe critical issue is that the comment on line 130 says \"This must be called regardless of whether defaults.custom exists,\" and indeed `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` is called unconditionally. However, **the migration is incomplete**: when no explicit `defaults.custom` exists in the input JSON, the frontend defaults logic later creates an empty `defaults.custom = {}` structure during the `applyPanelDefaults()` phase (which runs BEFORE v38 migration in the pipeline).\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Evidence 1: Migration Pipeline Order (migrate.go:51-135)\n\nThe execution order in `/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/migrate.go` is:\n\n1. **Line 71:** `trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom(dash)` - Marks panels that HAD custom in original input\n2. **Lines 73-75:** `applyFrontendDefaults(dash)` and `applyPanelDefaults(panel)` - Creates default fieldConfig structures, including empty `defaults.custom = {}` even if not in original\n3. **Lines 119-126:** Schema version migrations run (including **v38 at some version**)\n4. **Line 135:** `cleanupDashboardForSave(dash)` - Removes properties that weren't in original input\n\n### Evidence 2: The v38 Migration Gap (v38.go:115-131)\n\nThe v38 migration processes overrides (line 131) unconditionally, but there's a subtle problem:\n\n- When the dashboard was originally saved WITHOUT `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`, the tracking marker `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom` is NOT set\n- The `applyPanelDefaults()` creates an empty `defaults.custom = {}` to match frontend behavior\n- v38 migration runs and processes the overrides correctly\n- BUT: During the later `cleanupFieldConfigDefaults()` call in `cleanupDashboardForSave()`, the empty custom object gets removed because the cleanup logic doesn't recognize it as legitimate\n\n### Evidence 3: The Cleanup Logic (frontend_defaults.go:1032-1070)\n\n```go\nfunc cleanupFieldConfigDefaults(defaults map[string]interface{}, panel map[string]interface{}) {\n    // Remove empty custom objects from migrated singlestat panels (frontend filters them out)\n    if custom, exists := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{}); exists {\n        if len(custom) == 0 {\n            // Check if this is a migrated singlestat panel...\n            // If so, delete the custom object\n            if isMigratedSinglestat {\n                delete(defaults, \"custom\")\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis cleanup removes empty `custom` objects, but it only considers specific migrated singlestat scenarios. However, after v38 migration processes displayMode→cellOptions conversions, if a panel has NO OTHER field config properties beyond cellOptions in custom, the custom object may become empty after cleanup.\n\n### Evidence 4: Tracking Marker Preservation (frontend_defaults.go:666-671)\n\nThe preservation logic exists but is insufficient:\n\n```go\n// Preserve custom object if it was originally present, even if empty\nif panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\\\"] == true {\n    if _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; !hasCustom {\n        defaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis ONLY preserves the custom object if the marker was set in the original input. exists {\n        if len(custom) == 0 {\n            // Check if this is a migrated singlestat panel...\n            // If so, delete the custom object\n            if isMigratedSinglestat {\n                delete(defaults, \"custom\")\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis cleanup removes empty `custom` objects, but it only considers specific migrated singlestat scenarios. However, after v38 migration processes displayMode→cellOptions conversions, if a panel has NO OTHER field config properties beyond cellOptions in custom, the custom object may become empty after cleanup.\n\n### Evidence 4: Tracking Marker Preservation (frontend_defaults.go:666-671)\n\nThe preservation logic exists but is insufficient:\n\n```go\n// Preserve custom object if it was originally present, even if empty\nif panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\\\"] == true {\n    if _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; !hasCustom {\n        defaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis ONLY preserves the custom object if the marker was set in the original input. However:\n- Dashboards without explicit `defaults.custom` won't have the marker\n- After v38 migration adds cellOptions, if no other custom properties exist, the structure becomes valid but isn't recognized as needing preservation\n\n## Affected Components\n\n1. **apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go** (Lines 75-132)\n   - Function: `V38()` and `processPanelsV38()`\n   - Function: `migrateOverrides()` (Lines 135-176)\n   - Issue: Assumes the cleanup phase will correctly preserve cellOptions\n\n2. **apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go** (Lines 1032-1070)\n   - Function: `cleanupFieldConfigDefaults()`\n   - Issue: Doesn't recognize cellOptions in custom as a legitimate reason to preserve the custom object for non-singlestat panels\n\n3. **apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/migrate.go** (Lines 51-135)\n   - Function: `migrate()`\n   - Issue: Order-dependent behavior where tracking happens before defaults are applied, but v38 migration relies on defaults being present\n\n## Which Dashboard Schema Version Triggers the Issue\n\nThe regression affects dashboards with **schema versions 37 and below** being migrated to **v38 or later**, specifically:\n\n- Dashboards saved with v37 or earlier that had table panels\n- Table panels with `fieldConfig.overrides` containing `custom.displayMode` properties\n- **WITHOUT** explicit `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` object in the original JSON\n\n## Files and Functions Involved in Migration Chain\n\n```\nmigrate.go:51-135 - Main orchestrator\n├── migrate.go:67 - trackOriginalTransformations()\n├── migrate.go:71 - trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom()  [KEY]\n├── frontend_defaults.go:10-73 - applyFrontendDefaults()\n├── migrate.go:82 - applyPanelDefaults()             [Creates empty defaults.custom]\n├── migrate.go:104 - ensurePanelsHaveUniqueIds()\n├── migrate.go:119-127 - Version migration loop\n│   └── schemaversion/v38.go:75-132 - cleanupFieldConfigDefaults()  [REMOVES cellOptions]\n```\n\n## Recommendation\n\nThe fix requires updating the `cleanupFieldConfigDefaults()` function to recognize and preserve `defaults.custom` objects that contain `cellOptions`, as these are legitimate field configurations created by the v38 migration and should not be considered \"empty\" or eligible for removal.\n\nAlternative approach: Modify v38 migration to ensure the tracking marker `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom` is set after processing cellOptions conversions, so the preservation logic can properly maintain the custom object.\n\nThe core issue: **The cleanup phase doesn't know that cellOptions in custom is a legitimate, non-default field configuration that must be preserved**, resulting in silent loss of table panel formatting after v38 migration for dashboards without explicit original `defaults.custom`.\n","numLines":140,"startLine":1,"totalLines":140}}}
